Create bus index mapping\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = y\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Off-nominal tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef power_flow_equations(x, buses, generators, gencosts, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute power balance mismatches\n    x = [Vm_1, ..., Vm_n, Va_1, ..., Va_n, Pg_1, ..., Pg_ng, Qg_1, ..., Qg_ng]\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Approximation\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        Pgpu = Pg[k]\n        PgMW = Pgpu * 100.0  # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_balance = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_balance = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Mismatches for PQ and PV buses (P) and PQ buses (Q)\n        mismatch = []\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return mismatch, cost, S_inj, S_gen, Vm, Va\n\n    # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost, constraints\n\n    # Simplified approach: Run power flow first, then economic dispatch\n    # For this implementation, we'll use a more practical approach\n\n    # Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost\n\n    def eq_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        cons = []\n\n        # Q balance constraints\n    for i, pq_idx in enumerate(PQ_buses):\n        offset = n_buses - 1  # Number of P constraints\n        cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, off=offset, pq=pq_idx: eq_constraints(s)[off + i]})\n\n    try:\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=bounds,\n            constraints=cons,\n            options={'ftol': 1e-9, 'maxiter': 1000, 'disp': False}\n        )\n\n        status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = result.x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = result.x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {e}\")\n        status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': status,\n        'x_opt': result.x if 'result' in dir() else x0\n    }\n\ndef compute_power_flow_solution(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Vm_init, Va_init):\n    \"\"\"Run power flow to get consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # Verify feasibility\n    # Compute power mismatches\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * solution['Va'] * np.pi / 180)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator injection at buses\n    S_gen = np.zeros(len(buses), dtype=complex)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n    P_mismatch = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_mismatch = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n    max_P_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n    max_Q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    v_violations = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] or Vm[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            v_violations.append(abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmin']) if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] else abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmax']))\n    max_v_violation = max(v_violations) if v_violations else 0.0\n\n    # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # Convert to p.u.\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Gs': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Bs': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmax': row[11],\n            'vmin': row[12]\n        }\n        buses.append(bus)\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_list, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Parse generator data - format: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, Pc1, Pc2]\"\"\"\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_list):\n        gen = {\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(row[0]),\n            'Pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'Qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Vg': row[5],\n            'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA\n        }\n        generators.append(gen)\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_list):\n    \"\"\"Parse generator cost data - format: [model, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0]\"\"\"\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        model = int(row[0])\n        n = int(row[3])\n        if model == 2:  # Polynomial\n            # Cost coefficients: c2, c1, c0 (or fewer if n < 3)\n            c = [float(row[4 + i]) for i in range(n)]\n            costs.append({'model': model, 'n': n, 'c': c})\n        else:\n            costs.append({'model': model, 'n': n, 'c': []})\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_list, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Parse branch data - format: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\"\"\"\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branch = {\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': row[4],\n            'rateA': row[5] / baseMVA,  # MVA limit in p.u.\n            'rateB': row[6] / baseMVA,\n            'rateC': row[7] / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],  # Transformer tap ratio\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180,  # Convert to radians\n            'angmin': row[11] * np.pi / 180,\n            'angmax': row[12] * np.pi / 180\n        }\n        branches.append(branch)\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = y\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Off-nominal tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef power_flow_equations(x, buses, generators, gencosts, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute power balance mismatches\n    x = [Vm_1, ..., Vm_n, Va_1, ..., Va_n, Pg_1, ..., Pg_ng, Qg_1, ..., Qg_ng]\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vm = x[:n_buses]\n    Va = x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n    Qg = x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n    # Build voltage vector\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n\n    # Compute complex power injection\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    # Compute load\n    S_load = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        S_load[i] = bus['Pd'] + 1j * bus['Qd']\n\n    # Compute generator injection\n    S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n    # Power mismatch (excluding reference bus)\n    P_mismatch = []\n    Q_mismatch = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        P_calc = S_inj[i].real + S_load[i].real - S_gen[i].real\n        Q_calc = S_inj[i].imag + S_load[i].imag - S_gen[i].imag\n\n        if bus['type'] == 3:  # Reference bus - don't include Q mismatch\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:  # PV bus - don't include Q mismatch\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n        else:  # PQ bus\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n    return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\ndef cost_function(x, generators, gencosts):\n    \"\"\"Compute total generation cost\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(x) // 2  # Approximation\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        Pgpu = Pg[k]\n        PgMW = Pgpu * 100.0  # Convert back to MW for cost calc\n\n        # Polynomial cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW**2 + cost['c'][1] * PgMW + cost['c'][2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW + cost['c'][1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 1:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0]\n\n    return total_cost\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using sequential quadratic programming\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    # Initial guess\n    Vm_init = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va_init = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Pg_init = np.array([gen['Pg'] for gen in generators])\n    Qg_init = np.array([gen['Qg'] for gen in generators])\n\n    # Set reference bus angle to 0\n    Va_init[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initial state vector (excluding reference bus angles and Q at PV buses)\n    # We'll handle constraints differently\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init])\n\n    # Create masks for variable indices\n    Va_mask = np.ones(n_buses, dtype=bool)\n    Va_mask[ref_bus_idx] = False\n\n    PV_buses = [i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 2]\n    PQ_buses = [i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 1]\n\n    # Function to extract variables and compute mismatches\n    def mismatch_and_cost(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_balance = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_balance = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Mismatches for PQ and PV buses (P) and PQ buses (Q)\n        mismatch = []\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                mismatch.append(P_balance[i])\n\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            mismatch.append(Q_balance[i])\n\n        mismatch = np.array(mismatch)\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return mismatch, cost, S_inj, S_gen, Vm, Va\n\n    # Use Newton-Raphson power flow as starting point, then optimize\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the initial power flow solution and adjust dispatch\n\n    def objective_and_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            constraints.append({\n                'type': 'eq',\n                'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.imag(S_inj[idx]) + Qd[idx] - np.imag(S_gen[idx])\n            })\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost, constraints\n\n    # Simplified approach: Run power flow first, then economic dispatch\n    # For this implementation, we'll use a more practical approach\n\n    # Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost\n\n    def eq_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        cons = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                cons.append(np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i]))\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            cons.append(np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i]))\n\n        return np.array(cons)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Vm bounds\n    for bus in buses:\n        bounds.append((bus['vmin'], bus['vmax']))\n\n    # Va bounds (exclude reference)\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i == ref_bus_idx:\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))  # Reference angle fixed\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Pg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n    # Qg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Qmin'], gen['Qmax']))\n\n    # Constraint definitions for SLSQP\n    cons = []\n\n    # P balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i != ref_bus_idx:\n            cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, idx=i: eq_constraints(s)[idx])\n\n    # Q balance constraints\n    for i, pq_idx in enumerate(PQ_buses):\n        offset = n_buses - 1  # Number of P constraints\n        cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, off=offset, pq=pq_idx: eq_constraints(s)[off + i]})\n\n    try:\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=bounds,\n            constraints=cons,\n            options={'ftol': 1e-9, 'maxiter': 1000, 'disp': False}\n        )\n\n        status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = result.x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = result.x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {e}\")\n        status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': status,\n        'x_opt': result.x if 'result' in dir() else x0\n    }\n\ndef compute_power_flow_solution(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Vm_init, Va_init):\n    \"\"\"Run power flow to get consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # Verify feasibility\n    # Compute power mismatches\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * solution['Va'] * np.pi / 180)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator injection at buses\n    S_gen = np.zeros(len(buses), dtype=complex)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n    P_mismatch = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_mismatch = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n    max_P_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n    max_Q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    v_violations = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] or Vm[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            v_violations.append(abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmin']) if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] else abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmax']))\n    max_v_violation = max(v_violations) if v_violations else 0.0\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    overloads = []\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['flow_from_MVA'] > bf['limit_MVA']:\n            overloads.append(bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n    max_overload = max(overloads) if overloads else 0.0\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_losses, 2),\n            \"solver_status\": solution['status']\n        },\n        \"generators\": [],\n        \"buses\": [],\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": [],\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": round(max_P_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": round(max_Q_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": round(max_v_violation, 6),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": round(max_overload, 6)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Add generators\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        report[\"generators\"].append({\n            \"id\": gen['id'],\n            \"bus\": gen['bus'],\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg[k], 4),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg[k], 4),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['Pmax'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 4)\n        })\n\n    # Add buses\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        report[\"buses\"].append({\n            \"id\": bus['id'],\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm[i], 6),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va[i], 4),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['vmax']\n        })\n\n    # Sort branches by loading and add top 10 most loaded\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)[:10]\n    for bf in sorted_branches:\n        report[\"most_loaded_branches\"].append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(bf['from_bus']),\n            \"to_bus\": int(bf['to_bus']),\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bf['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bf['flow_from_MVA'], 4),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bf['flow_to_MVA'], 4),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bf['limit_MVA'], 4)\n        })\n\n    # Write report\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"AC OPF SOLUTION COMPLETE\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_Pg:.2f} MW, {total_Qg:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_Pd:.2f} MW, {total_Qd:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {total_losses:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Solver Status: {solution['status']}\")\n    print(f\"\\nFeasibility:\")\n    print(f\"  Max P mismatch: {max_P_mismatch:.6f} MW\")\n    print(f\"  Max Q mismatch: {max_Q_mismatch:.6f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"  Max voltage violation: {max_v_violation:.6f} pu\")\n    print(f\"  Max branch overload: {max_overload:.6f} MVA\")\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to /root/report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Approximation\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        Pgpu = Pg[k]\n        PgMW = Pgpu * 100.0  # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    # Initial guess\n    Vm_init = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va_init = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Pg_init = np.array([gen['Pg'] for gen in generators])\n    Qg_init = np.array([gen['Qg'] for gen in generators])\n\n    # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_balance = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_balance = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Mismatches for PQ and PV buses (P) and PQ buses (Q)\n        mismatch = []\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                mismatch.append(P_balance[i])\n\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            mismatch.append(Q_balance[i])\n\n        mismatch = np.array(mismatch)\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return mismatch, cost, S_inj, S_gen, Vm, Va\n\n    # Use Newton-Raphson power flow as starting point, then optimize\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the initial power flow solution and adjust dispatch\n\n    def objective_and_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            constraints.append({\n                'type': 'eq',\n                'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.imag(S_inj[idx]) + Qd[idx] - np.imag(S_gen[idx])\n            })\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost, constraints\n\n    # Simplified approach: Run power flow first, then economic dispatch\n    # For this implementation, we'll use a more practical approach\n\n    # Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost\n\n    def eq_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        cons = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                cons.append(np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i]))\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            cons.append(np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i]))\n\n        return np.array(cons)\n\n    # Constraint definitions for SLSQP\n    cons = []\n\n    # P balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i != ref_bus_idx:\n            cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, idx=i: eq_constraints(s)[idx])\n\n    # Q balance constraints\n    for i, pq_idx in enumerate(PQ_buses):\n        offset = n_buses - 1  # Number of P constraints\n        cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, off=offset, pq=pq_idx: eq_constraints(s)[off + i]})\n\n    try:\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=bounds,\n            constraints=cons,\n            options={'ftol': 1e-9, 'maxiter': 1000, 'disp': False}\n        )\n\n        status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = result.x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = result.x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {e}\")\n        status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': status,\n        'x_opt': result.x if 'result' in dir() else x0\n    }\n\ndef compute_power_flow_solution(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Vm_init, Va_init):\n    \"\"\"Run power flow to get consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # Verify feasibility\n    # Compute power mismatches\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * solution['Va'] * np.pi / 180)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator injection at buses\n    S_gen = np.zeros(len(buses), dtype=complex)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n    P_mismatch = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_mismatch = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n    max_P_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n    max_Q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    v_violations = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] or Vm[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            v_violations.append(abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmin']) if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] else abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmax']))\n    max_v_violation = max(v_violations) if v_violations else 0.0\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    overloads = []\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['flow_from_MVA'] > bf['limit_MVA']:\n            overloads.append(bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n    max_overload = max(overloads) if overloads else 0.0\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_losses, 2),\n            \"solver_status\": solution['status']\n        },\n        \"generators\": [],\n        \"buses\": [],\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": [],\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": round(max_P_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": round(max_Q_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": round(max_v_violation, 6),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": round(max_overload, 6)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Add buses\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        report[\"buses\"].append({\n            \"id\": bus['id'],\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm[i], 6),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va[i], 4),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['vmax']\n        })\n\n    # Sort branches by loading and add top 10 most loaded\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)[:10]\n    for bf in sorted_branches:\n        report[\"most_loaded_branches\"].append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(bf['from_bus']),\n            \"to_bus\": int(bf['to_bus']),\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bf['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bf['flow_from_MVA'], 4),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bf['flow_to_MVA'], 4),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bf['limit_MVA'], 4)\n        })\n\n    # Regular line (tap = 1.0)\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series\n\n    return ybus","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC OPF) Solver\nBased on the mathematical model in math-model.md\nMinimizes generation cost subject to AC power flow constraints\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize\nfrom scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags\nimport cmath\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file (MATPOWER format)\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data - format: [id, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, vm, va, baseKV, zone, vmax, vmin]\"\"\"\n    buses = []\n    for row in bus_list:\n        bus = {\n            'id': int(row[0]),\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # Series admittance\n        y_series = y\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Off-nominal tap\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef power_flow_equations(x, buses, generators, gencosts, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute power balance mismatches\n    x = [Vm_1, ..., Vm_n, Va_1, ..., Va_n, Pg_1, ..., Pg_ng, Qg_1, ..., Qg_ng]\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Compute complex power injection\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    # Compute load\n    S_load = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        S_load[i] = bus['Pd'] + 1j * bus['Qd']\n\n    # Approximation\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        Pgpu = Pg[k]\n        PgMW = Pgpu * 100.0  # Convert back to MW for cost calc\n\n        # Polynomial cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW**2 + cost['c'][1] * PgMW + cost['c'][2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW + cost['c'][1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 1:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0]\n\n    return total_cost\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using sequential quadratic programming\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Set reference bus angle to 0\n    Va_init[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initial state vector (excluding reference bus angles and Q at PV buses)\n    # We'll handle constraints differently\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init])\n\n    # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_balance = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_balance = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Mismatches for PQ and PV buses (P) and PQ buses (Q)\n        mismatch = []\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                mismatch.append(P_balance[i])\n\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            mismatch.append(Q_balance[i])\n\n        mismatch = np.array(mismatch)\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return mismatch, cost, S_inj, S_gen, Vm, Va\n\n    # Use Newton-Raphson power flow as starting point, then optimize\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the initial power flow solution and adjust dispatch\n\n    def objective_and_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            constraints.append({\n                'type': 'eq',\n                'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.imag(S_inj[idx]) + Qd[idx] - np.imag(S_gen[idx])\n            })\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost, constraints\n\n    # Simplified approach: Run power flow first, then economic dispatch\n    # For this implementation, we'll use a more practical approach\n\n    # Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost\n\n    def eq_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        cons = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                cons.append(np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i]))\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            cons.append(np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i]))\n\n        return np.array(cons)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Vm bounds\n    for bus in buses:\n        bounds.append((bus['vmin'], bus['vmax']))\n\n    # Va bounds (exclude reference)\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i == ref_bus_idx:\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))  # Reference angle fixed\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Pg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n    # Qg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Qmin'], gen['Qmax']))\n\n    # Constraint definitions for SLSQP\n    cons = []\n\n    # P balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i != ref_bus_idx:\n            cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, idx=i: eq_constraints(s)[idx]})\n\n    # Q balance constraints\n    for i, pq_idx in enumerate(PQ_buses):\n        offset = n_buses - 1  # Number of P constraints\n        cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, off=offset, pq=pq_idx: eq_constraints(s)[off + i]})\n\n    try:\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=bounds,\n            constraints=cons,\n            options={'ftol': 1e-9, 'maxiter': 1000, 'disp': False}\n        )\n\n        status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = result.x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = result.x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {e}\")\n        status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': status,\n        'x_opt': result.x if 'result' in dir() else x0\n    }\n\ndef compute_power_flow_solution(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Vm_init, Va_init):\n    \"\"\"Run power flow to get consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # Verify feasibility\n    # Compute power mismatches\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * solution['Va'] * np.pi / 180)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Polynomial\n            # Cost coefficients: c2, c1, c0 (or fewer if n < 3)\n            c = [float(row[4 + i]) for i in range(n)]\n            costs.append({'model': model, 'n': n, 'c': c})\n        else:\n            costs.append({'model': model, 'n': n, 'c': []})\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_list, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Parse branch data - format: [from, to, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\"\"\"\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branch = {\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': row[4],\n            'rateA': row[5] / baseMVA,  # MVA limit in p.u.\n            'rateB': row[6] / baseMVA,\n            'rateC': row[7] / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],  # Transformer tap ratio\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180,  # Convert to radians\n            'angmin': row[11] * np.pi / 180,\n            'angmax': row[12] * np.pi / 180\n        }\n        branches.append(branch)\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = y\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Handle transformer with tap ratio\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series / tap\n        else:\n            # Regular line (tap = 1.0)\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef power_flow_equations(x, buses, generators, gencosts, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute power balance mismatches\n    x = [Vm_1, ..., Vm_n, Va_1, ..., Va_n, Pg_1, ..., Pg_ng, Qg_1, ..., Qg_ng]\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    #
