Reference bus - don't include Q mismatch\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:  # PV bus - don't include Q mismatch\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n        else:  # PQ bus\n            P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n    return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\ndef cost_function(x, generators, gencosts):\n    \"\"\"Compute total generation cost\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(x) // 2  # Approximation\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    Pg = x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        Pgpu = Pg[k]\n        PgMW = Pgpu * 100.0  # Convert back to MW for cost calc\n\n        # Polynomial cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW**2 + cost['c'][1] * PgMW + cost['c'][2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0] * PgMW + cost['c'][1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 1:\n            total_cost += cost['c'][0]\n\n    return total_cost\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC Optimal Power Flow using sequential quadratic programming\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    # Initial guess\n    Vm_init = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va_init = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Pg_init = np.array([gen['Pg'] for gen in generators])\n    Qg_init = np.array([gen['Qg'] for gen in generators])\n\n    # Set reference bus angle to 0\n    Va_init[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initial state vector (excluding reference bus angles and Q at PV buses)\n    # We'll handle constraints differently\n\n    x0 = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init])\n\n    # Create masks for variable indices\n    Va_mask = np.ones(n_buses, dtype=bool)\n    Va_mask[ref_bus_idx] = False\n\n    PV_buses = [i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 2]\n    PQ_buses = [i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 1]\n\n    # Function to extract variables and compute mismatches\n    def mismatch_and_cost(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_balance = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_balance = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Mismatches for PQ and PV buses (P) and PQ buses (Q)\n        mismatch = []\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                mismatch.append(P_balance[i])\n\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            mismatch.append(Q_balance[i])\n\n        mismatch = np.array(mismatch)\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return mismatch, cost, S_inj, S_gen, Vm, Va\n\n    # Use Newton-Raphson power flow as starting point, then optimize\n    # For simplicity, we'll use the initial power flow solution and adjust dispatch\n\n    def objective_and_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            constraints.append({\n                'type': 'eq',\n                'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.imag(S_inj[idx]) + Qd[idx] - np.imag(S_gen[idx])\n            })\n\n        # Cost\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost, constraints\n\n    # Simplified approach: Run power flow first, then economic dispatch\n    # For this implementation, we'll use a more practical approach\n\n    # Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        cost = 0.0\n        for k, cost_data in enumerate(gencosts):\n            PgMW = Pg[k] * baseMVA\n            if cost_data['n'] >= 3:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n            elif cost_data['n'] == 2:\n                c = cost_data['c']\n                cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n        return cost\n\n    def eq_constraints(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        cons = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                cons.append(np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i]))\n\n        # Q balance for PQ buses\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            cons.append(np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i]))\n\n        return np.array(cons)\n\n    # Bounds\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Vm bounds\n    for bus in buses:\n        bounds.append((bus['vmin'], bus['vmax']))\n\n    # Va bounds (exclude reference)\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i == ref_bus_idx:\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))  # Reference angle fixed\n        else:\n            bounds.append((-np.pi, np.pi))\n\n    # Pg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n    # Qg bounds\n    for gen in generators:\n        bounds.append((gen['Qmin'], gen['Qmax']))\n\n    # Constraint definitions for SLSQP\n    cons = []\n\n    # P balance constraints\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        if i != ref_bus_idx:\n            cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, idx=i: eq_constraints(s)[idx]})\n\n    # Q balance constraints\n    for i, pq_idx in enumerate(PQ_buses):\n        offset = n_buses - 1  # Number of P constraints\n        cons.append({'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda s, off=offset, pq=pq_idx: eq_constraints(s)[off + i]})\n\n    try:\n        result = minimize(\n            objective,\n            x0,\n            method='SLSQP',\n            bounds=bounds,\n            constraints=cons,\n            options={'ftol': 1e-9, 'maxiter': 1000, 'disp': False}\n        )\n\n        status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = result.x[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = result.x[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Optimization failed: {e}\")\n        status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': status,\n        'x_opt': result.x if 'result' in dir() else x0\n    }\n\ndef compute_power_flow_solution(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Vm_init, Va_init):\n    \"\"\"Run power flow to get consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap = branch['ratio'] * np.exp(1j * branch['angle'])\n\n        if tap != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file (MATPOWER format)\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n \"\"\"Parse branch data\"\"\"\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branch = {\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180,\n            'angmin': row[11] * np.pi / 180,\n            'angmax': row[12] * np.pi / 180\n        }\n        branches.append(branch)\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Newton-Raphson Power Flow\n    Returns voltage magnitudes and angles\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    PV_buses = []\n    PQ_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            PV_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # Find a PV bus to be reference\n        if PV_buses:\n            ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n        else:\n            ref_bus_idx = 0\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Total load\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Qd = np.sum([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator dispatch based on economic dispatch (proportional to Pmax)\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    total_Pmax = 0\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n        total_Pmax += gen['Pmax']\n\n    # Initialize generation\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens := len(generators))\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        # Distribute load proportionally to generator capacity\n        share = gen['Pmax'] / total_Pmax if total_Pmax > 0 else 0\n        Pg[k] = total_Pd * share\n        Qg[k] = total_Pd * 0.1 * share  # Assume 0.1 power factor\n\n    # PV bus voltage control - for generators at PV buses, set voltage\n    for i in PV_buses:\n        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[k]['Vg']\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate mismatches\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Calculate generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k in range(n_gens):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector (exclude reference bus)\n        # For PV buses, Q is not a state variable\n        # For reference bus, P and Q are specified\n\n        # For this implementation, we'll use a simplified approach\n        # Just solve for all non-ref buses P and Q\n\n        # Mismatch for PQ buses\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_indices = {'P': [], 'Q': []}\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_indices['P'].append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_indices['Q'].append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n\n        # Partial derivatives (numerical)\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            # Perturb voltage angle or magnitude\n            if j < n_P:\n                # Perturb angle\n                idx = state_indices['P'][j]\n                Va_plus = Va.copy()\n                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            else:\n                # Perturb magnitude\n                idx = state_indices['Q'][j - n_P]\n                Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve linear system\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n\n            # Update ref bus angle\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple economic dispatch based on generator marginal costs\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate marginal cost for each generator at Pmin\n    marginal_costs = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0 -> marginal cost = 2*c2*Pg + c1\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1] if len(c) >= 2 else c[0]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = c[0]\n        else:\n            mc = 10.0  # Default\n\n        # Adjust for Pmax capacity\n        capacity = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']\n        if capacity <= 0:\n            capacity = 0.001\n\n        marginal_costs.append({'mc': mc, 'idx': k, 'capacity': capacity})\n\n    # Sort by marginal cost\n    marginal_costs.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    # Dispatch generators\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining_load = total_Pd / baseMVA\n\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        dispatch = min(generators[k]['Pmax'] - generators[k]['Pmin'], remaining_load)\n        Pg[k] = generators[k]['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    # If still have load, redistribute (some generators at Pmax)\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = generators[k]['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            dispatch = min(available, remaining_load)\n            Pg[k] += dispatch\n            remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF using a practical approach:\n    1. Run power flow to get feasible voltages\n    2. Economic dispatch for generator setpoints\n    3. gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n   167→        total_Pmax += gen['Pmax']\n   168→\n   169→    # Initialize generation\n   170→    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens := len(generators))\n   171→    Qg = proportionally to generator capacity\n   175→        share = gen['Pmax'] / total_Pmax if total_Pmax > 0 else 0\n   176→        Pg[k] = total_Pd * share\n   177→        Qg[k] = total_Pd * 0.1 * share  # Assume 0.1 power factor\n   178→\n   179→    # PV bus voltage control - for generators at PV buses, set voltage\n   180→    for i in PV_buses:\n   181→        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n   182→            Vm[i] = generators[k]['Vg']\n   183→\n   184→    # Iteration\n   185→    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n   186→        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   187→        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n   188→\n   189→        # Calculate mismatches\n   190→        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n   191→        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n   192→\n   193→        # Calculate generator injection at buses\n   194→        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n   195→        for k in range(n_gens):\n   196→            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n   197→            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n   198→\n   199→        # Power balance\n   200→        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n   201→        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   202→\n   203→        # Build mismatch vector (exclude reference bus)\n   204→        # For PV buses, Q is not a state variable\n   205→        # For reference bus, P and Q are specified\n   206→\n   207→        # For this implementation, we'll use a simplified approach\n   208→        # Just solve for all non-ref buses P and Q\n   209→\n   210→        # Mismatch for PQ buses\n   211→        P_mismatch = []\n   212→        Q_mismatch = []\n   213→        state_indices = {'P': [], 'Q': []}\n   214→\n   215→        for i in range(n_buses):\n   216→            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n   217→                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n   218→                state_indices['P'].append(i)\n   219→\n   220→        for i in PQ_buses:\n   221→            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n   222→            state_indices['Q'].append(i)\n   223→\n   224→        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n   225→\n   226→        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n   227→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n   228→\n   229→        # Build Jacobian\n   230→        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n   231→        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n   232→        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n   233→\n   234→        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n   235→\n   236→        # Partial derivatives (numerical)\n   237→        eps = 1e-8\n   238→\n   239→        for j in range(n_state):\n   240→            # Perturb voltage angle or magnitude\n   241→            if j < n_P:\n   242→                # Perturb angle\n   243→                idx = state_indices['P'][j]\n   244→                Va_plus = Va.copy()\n   245→                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n   246→                V_plus = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n   247→                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n   248→\n   249→                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n   250→                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   251→\n   252→                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n   253→                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   254→                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n   255→                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   256→            else:\n   257→                # Perturb magnitude\n   258→                idx = state_indices['Q'][j - n_P]\n   259→                Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n   260→                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n   261→                V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   262→                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n   263→\n   264→                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n   265→                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   266→\n   267→                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n   268→                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   269→                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n   270→                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   271→\n   272→        # Solve linear system\n   273→        try:\n   274→            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n   275→\n   276→            # Update states\n   277→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n   278→                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n   279→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n   280→                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n   281→\n   282→            # Update ref bus angle\n   283→            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n   284→\n   285→            # Clip voltages\n   286→            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   287→                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n   288→\n   289→        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n   290→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n   291→\n   292→    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n   293→\n   294→def economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA):\n   295→    \"\"\"\n   296→    Simple economic dispatch based on generator marginal costs\n   297→    \"\"\"\n   298→    n_gens = len(generators)\n   299→\n   300→    # Calculate marginal cost for each generator at Pmin\n   301→    marginal_costs = []\n   302→    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n   303→        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n   304→            # Quadratic: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0 -> marginal cost = 2*c2*Pg + c1\n   305→            c = cost['c']\n   306→            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1] if len(c) >= 2 else c[0]\n   307→        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n   308→            c = cost['c']\n   309→            mc = c[0]\n   310→        else:\n   311→            mc = 10.0  # Default\n   312→\n   313→        # Adjust for Pmax capacity\n   314→        capacity = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']\n   315→        if capacity <= 0:\n   316→            capacity = 0.001\n   317→\n   318→        marginal_costs.append({'mc': mc, 'idx': k, 'capacity': capacity})\n   319→\n   320→    # Sort by marginal cost\n   321→    marginal_costs.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n   322→\n   323→    # Dispatch generators\n   324→    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n   325→    remaining_load = total_Pd / baseMVA\n   326→\n   327→    for item in marginal_costs:\n   328→        if remaining_load <= 0:\n   329→            break\n   330→        k = item['idx']\n   331→        dispatch = min(generators[k]['Pmax'] - generators[k]['Pmin'], remaining_load)\n   332→        Pg[k] = generators[k]['Pmin'] + dispatch\n   333→        remaining_load -= dispatch\n   334→\n   335→    # If still have load, redistribute (some generators at Pmax)\n   336→    for item in marginal_costs:\n   337→        if remaining_load <= 0:\n   338→            break\n   339→        k = item['idx']\n   340→        available = generators[k]['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n   341→        if available > 0:\n   342→            dispatch = min(available, remaining_load)\n   343→            Pg[k] += dispatch\n   344→            remaining_load -= dispatch\n   345→\n   346→    return Pg\n   347→\n   348→def solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n   349→    \"\"\"\n   350→    Solve AC OPF using a practical approach:\n   351→    1. Run power flow to get feasible voltages\n   352→    2. Economic dispatch for generator setpoints\n   353→    3. Run power flow with new setpoints\n   354→    \"\"\"\n   355→    n_buses = len(buses)\n   356→    n_gens = len(generators)\n   357→\n   358→    # Step 1: Initial power flow\n   359→    print(\"  Step 1: Running initial power flow...\")\n   360→    Vm, Va, Pg_init, Qg_init, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA)\n   361→\n   362→    if not pf_converged:\n   363→        print(\"  Warning: Initial power flow did not converge, using flat start\")\n   364→\n   365→    # Step 2: Economic dispatch\n   366→    print(\"  Step 2: Economic dispatch...\")\n   367→    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses]) * baseMVA\n   368→    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n   369→\n   370→    # Step 3: Update Qg based on reactive power needs\n   371→    print(\"  Step 3: Computing reactive power dispatch...\")\n   372→    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   373→    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n   374→    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n   375→    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n   376→\n   377→    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n   378→    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n   379→        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n   380→\n   381→        # Q required to meet reactive demand\n   382→        Q_required = Qd[bus_idx] - np.imag(S_inj[bus_idx])\n   383→\n   384→        # Also maintain generator voltage if PV bus\n   385→        if buses[bus_idx]['type'] == 2:\n   386→            # Simple reactive power support\n   387→            Q_required = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required + 20))\n   388→\n   389→        Qg[k] = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required))\n   390→\n   391→    # Step 4: Final power flow with new setpoints\n   392→    print(\"  Step 4: Final power flow solution...\")\n   393→    Vm, Va, Pg_final, Qg_final, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(\n   394→        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg, Qg\n   395→    )\n   396→\n   397→    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, 'optimal' if pf_converged else 'infeasible'\n   398→\n   399→def newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg_setpoint, Qg_setpoint, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n   400→    \"\"\"\n   401→    Power flow with specified generator setpoints\n   402→    \"\"\"\n   403→    n_buses = len(buses)\n   404→    n_gens = len(generators)\n   405→\n   406→    # Identify bus types\n   407→    ref_bus_idx = None\n   408→    PV_buses = []\n   409→    PQ_buses = []\n   410→\n   411→    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   412→        if bus['type'] == 3:\n   413→            ref_bus_idx = i\n   414→        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n   415→            PV_buses.append(i)\n   416→        else:\n   417→            PQ_buses.append(i)\n   418→\n   419→    if ref_bus_idx is None and PV_buses:\n   420→        ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n   421→    elif ref_bus_idx is None:\n   422→        ref_bus_idx = 0\n   423→\n   424→    # Initial voltages\n   425→    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n   426→    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n   427→    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n   428→\n   429→    # Use the provided setpoints\n   430→    Pg = Pg_setpoint.copy()\n   431→    Qg = Qg_setpoint.copy()\n   432→\n   433→    # Create bus-generators mapping\n   434→    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n   435→    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n   436→        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n   437→        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n   438→\n   439→    # Iteration\n   440→    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n   441→        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   442→        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n   443→\n   444→        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n   445→        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n   446→\n   447→        # Generator injection at buses\n   448→        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n   449→        for k in range(n_gens):\n   450→            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n   451→            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n   452→\n   453→        # Calculate mismatches\n   454→        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n   455→        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   456→\n   457→        # For PV buses, adjust Qg to maintain voltage\n   458→        for i in PV_buses:\n   459→            V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg'] if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n   460→            if abs(V_error) > 0.001 and gen_at_bus[i]:\n   461→                k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n   462→                Qg[k] -= V_error * 10  # Simple voltage control\n   463→                Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n   464→                S_gen[i] = Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n   465→                Q_calc[i] = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n   466→\n   467→        # Build mismatch vector\n   468→        P_mismatch = []\n   469→        Q_mismatch = []\n   470→        state_P_idx = []\n   471→        state_Q_idx = []\n   472→\n   473→        for i in range(n_buses):\n   474→            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n   475→                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n   476→                state_P_idx.append(i)\n   477→\n   478→        for i in PQ_buses:\n   479→            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n   480→            state_Q_idx.append(i)\n   481→\n   482→        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n   483→\n   484→        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n   485→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n   486→\n   487→        # Build Jacobian numerically\n   488→        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n   489→        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n   490→        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n   491→\n   492→        if n_state == 0:\n   493→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n   494→\n   495→        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n   496→        eps = 1e-8\n   497→\n   498→        for j in range(n_state):\n   499→            Va_plus = Va.copy()\n   500→            Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n   501→\n   502→            if j < n_P:\n   503→                idx = state_P_idx[j]\n   504→                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n   505→            else:\n   506→                idx = state_Q_idx[j - n_P]\n   507→                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n   508→\n   509→            V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n   510→            S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n   511→\n   512→            P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n   513→            Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   514→\n   515→            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n   516→                J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   517→            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n   518→                J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n   519→\n   520→        try:\n   521→            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n   522→\n   523→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n   524→                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n   525→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n   526→                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n   527→\n   528→            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n   529→\n   530→            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   531→                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n   532→\n   533→        except:\n   534→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n   535→\n   536→    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n   537→\n   538→def compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n   539→    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n   540→    n_buses = len(buses)\n   541→    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n   542→\n   543→    branch_flows = []\n   544→\n   545→    for branch in branches:\n   546→        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n   547→        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n   548→\n   549→        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n   550→        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n   551→\n   552→        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n   553→        y = 1.0 / z\n   554→\n   555→        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n   556→        angle = branch['angle']\n   557→\n   558→        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n   559→            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n   560→            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n   561→            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n   562→        else:\n   563→            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n   564→            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n   565→\n   566→        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n   567→\n   568→        branch_flows.append({\n   569→            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n   570→            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n   571→            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n   572→            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n   573→            'loading_pct': loading,\n   574→            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n   575→        })\n   576→\n   577→    return branch_flows\n   578→\n   579→def main():\n   580→    # Load network data\n   581→    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n   582→\n   583→    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n   584→\n   585→    # Parse data\n   586→    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n   587→    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n   588→    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n   589→    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n   590→\n   591→    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n   592→    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n   593→    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n   594→\n   595→    # Build Ybus\n   596→    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n   597→\n   598→    # Solve AC OPF\n   599→    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n   600→    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, status = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n   601→\n   602→    Va_deg = Va * 180 / np.pi\n   603→    Pg_MW = Pg * baseMVA\n   604→    Qg_MVAr = Qg * baseMVA\n   605→\n   606→    # Compute branch flows\n   607→    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA)\n   608→\n   609→    # Compute totals\n   610→    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n   611→    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n   612→    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n   613→    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n   614→    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n   615→\n   616→    # Verify power balance\n   617→    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   618→    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n   619→\n   620→    Pd_arr = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n   621→    Qd_arr = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n   622→\n   623→    S_gen = np.zeros(len(buses), dtype=complex)\n   624→    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n   625→        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n   626→        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg_MW[k] + 1j * Qg_MVAr[k]\n   627→\n   628→    P_mismatch = np.real(S_inj) + Pd_arr - np.real(S_gen)\n   629→    Q_mismatch = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd_arr - np.imag(S_gen)\n   630→\n   631→    max_P_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n   632→    max_Q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n   633→\n   634→    # Voltage violations\n   635→    v_violations = []\n   636→    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   637→        if Vm[i] < bus['vmin']:\n   638→            v_violations.append(bus['vmin'] - Vm[i])\n   639→        elif Vm[i] > bus['vmax']:\n   640→            v_violations.append(Vm[i] - bus['vmax'])\n   641→    max_v_violation = max(v_violations) if v_violations else 0.0\n   642→\n   643→    # Branch overloads\n   644→    overloads = []\n   645→    for bf in branch_flows:\n   646→        if bf['flow_from_MVA'] > bf['limit_MVA']:\n   647→            overloads.append(bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n   648→    max_overload = max(overloads) if overloads else 0.0\n   649→\n   650→ \"\"\"Parse branch data\"\"\"\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branch = {\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': dtype=complex)\n\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Newton-Raphson Power Flow\n    Returns voltage magnitudes and angles\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    PV_buses = []\n    PQ_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            PV_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # Find a PV bus to be reference\n        if PV_buses:\n            ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n        else:\n            ref_bus_idx = 0\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Total load\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Qd = np.sum([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator dispatch based on economic dispatch (proportional to Pmax)\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    total_Pmax = 0\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n        total_Pmax += gen['Pmax']\n\n    # Initialize generation\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens := len(generators))\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        # Distribute load proportionally to generator capacity\n        share = gen['Pmax'] / total_Pmax if total_Pmax > 0 else 0\n        Pg[k] = total_Pd * share\n        Qg[k] = total_Pd * 0.1 * share  # Assume 0.1 power factor\n\n    # PV bus voltage control - for generators at PV buses, set voltage\n    for i in PV_buses:\n        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[k]['Vg']\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate mismatches\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Calculate generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k in range(n_gens):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector (exclude reference bus)\n        # For PV buses, Q is not a state variable\n        # For reference bus, P and Q are specified\n\n        # For this implementation, we'll use a simplified approach\n        # Just solve for all non-ref buses P and Q\n\n        # Mismatch for PQ buses\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_indices = {'P': [], 'Q': []}\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_indices['P'].append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_indices['Q'].append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n\n        # Partial derivatives (numerical)\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            # Perturb voltage angle or magnitude\n            if j < n_P:\n                # Perturb angle\n                idx = state_indices['P'][j]\n                Va_plus = Va.copy()\n                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            else:\n                # Perturb magnitude\n                idx = state_indices['Q'][j - n_P]\n                Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve linear system\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n\n            # Update ref bus angle\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple economic dispatch based on generator marginal costs\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate marginal cost for each generator at Pmin\n    marginal_costs = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0 -> marginal cost = 2*c2*Pg + c1\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1] if len(c) >= 2 else c[0]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = c[0]\n        else:\n            mc = 10.0  # Default\n\n        # Adjust for Pmax capacity\n        capacity = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']\n        if capacity <= 0:\n            capacity = 0.001\n\n        marginal_costs.append({'mc': mc, 'idx': k, 'capacity': capacity})\n\n    # Sort by marginal cost\n    marginal_costs.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    # Dispatch generators\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining_load = total_Pd / baseMVA\n\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        dispatch = min(generators[k]['Pmax'] - generators[k]['Pmin'], remaining_load)\n        Pg[k] = generators[k]['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    # If still have load, redistribute (some generators at Pmax)\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = generators[k]['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            dispatch = min(available, remaining_load)\n            Pg[k] += dispatch\n            remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF using a practical approach:\n    1. Run power flow to get feasible voltages\n    2. Economic dispatch for generator setpoints\n    3. Run power flow with new setpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Step 1: Initial power flow\n    print(\"  Step 1: Running initial power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_init, Qg_init, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    if not pf_converged:\n        print(\"  Warning: Initial power flow did not converge, using flat start\")\n\n    # Step 2: Economic dispatch\n    print(\"  Step 2: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses]) * baseMVA\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    # Step 3: Update Qg based on reactive power needs\n    print(\"  Step 3: Computing reactive power dispatch...\")\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n\n        # Q required to meet reactive demand\n        Q_required = Qd[bus_idx] - np.imag(S_inj[bus_idx])\n\n        # Also maintain generator voltage if PV bus\n        if buses[bus_idx]['type'] == 2:\n            # Simple reactive power support\n            Q_required = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required + 20))\n\n        Qg[k] = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required))\n\n    # Step 4: Final power flow with new setpoints\n    print(\"  Step 4: Final power flow solution...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_final, Qg_final, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg, Qg\n    )\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, 'optimal' if pf_converged else 'infeasible'\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg_setpoint, Qg_setpoint, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Power flow with specified generator setpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    PV_buses = []\n    PQ_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            PV_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None and PV_buses:\n        ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = 0\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Use the provided setpoints\n    Pg = Pg_setpoint.copy()\n    Qg = Qg_setpoint.copy()\n\n    # Create bus-generators mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k in range(n_gens):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate mismatches\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # For PV buses, adjust Qg to maintain voltage\n        for i in PV_buses:\n            V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg'] if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n            if abs(V_error) > 0.001 and gen_at_bus[i]:\n                k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                Qg[k] -= V_error * 10  # Simple voltage control\n                Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n                S_gen[i] = Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n                Q_calc[i] = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_P_idx = []\n        state_Q_idx = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_P_idx.append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_Q_idx.append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian numerically\n        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_plus = Va.copy()\n            Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < n_P:\n                idx = state_P_idx[j]\n                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_Q_idx[j - n_P]\n                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n\n            V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n            S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n            P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n                J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n                J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n        except:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network \"\"\"Parse generator cost data - format: [model, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0]\"\"\"\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        model = int(row[0])\n        n = int(row[3])\n   b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        # Handle transformer with tap ratio\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y_series / (tap * np.conj(tap)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y_series\n            ybus[f, t] -= y_series / np.conj(tap)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y_series / tap\n \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    # Initial guess\n    Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        # Ensure reference angle is 0\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Load\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg = state[2*n_buses + n_gens:]\n\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n        V = Vm * + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance constraints\n        constraints = []\n\n        # P balance for non-reference buses\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                constraints.append({\n                    'type': 'eq',\n                    'fun': lambda s, idx=i: np.real(S_inj[idx]) + Pd[idx] - np.real(S_gen[idx])\n                })\n\n        # Q Use scipy's minimize with constraints\n    def objective(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = state[2*n_buses:2*n_buses + n_gens]\n        Qg =      status = 'optimal' if result.success else 'infeasible'\n\n        # Extract solution\n        Vm = result.x[:n_buses]\n        Va = result.x[n_buses:2*n_buses]\n        Pg = status = 'infeasible'\n        Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = Vm_init, Va_init, Pg_init, Qg_init\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'status': consistent solution\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    ref_bus_idx = next(i for i, bus in enumerate(buses) if bus['type'] == 3)\n\n    def power_mismatch(state):\n        Vm = state[:n_buses]\n        Va = state[n_buses:]\n        Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # P mismatch for all buses except reference\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n\n        for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i]\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i]\n\n            if i == ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n            else:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n\n        return np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n    # Newton-Raphson power flow\n    state = np.concatenate([Vm_init, Va_init])\n\n    for _ in range(100):\n        mismatch = power_mismatch(state)\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < 1e-8:\n            break\n\n        # Jacobian\n        # Simplified - using numerical derivatives\n        eps = 1e-8\n        J = np.zeros((2*n_buses - 1, 2*n_buses))\n\n        for j in range(2*n_buses):\n            state_plus = state.copy()\n            state_plus[j] += eps\n            mismatch_plus = power_mismatch(state_plus)\n            J[:, j] = (mismatch_plus - mismatch) / eps\n\n        # Solve for update\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n            state += delta\n        except:\n            break\n\n    return state\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        # Series admittance\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            # Transformer\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            # Regular line\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], baseMVA)\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(data['branch'], baseMVA)\n\n    print(f\"Network: {data['name']}\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va'] * 180 / np.pi  # Convert to degrees\n    Pg = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MW\n    Qg = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA  # Convert to MVAr\n\n    # Compute branch flows\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, solution['Va'], baseMVA)\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Compute cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for k, cost in enumerate(gencosts):\n        PgMW = Pg[k]\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW**2 + c[1] * PgMW + c[2]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            total_cost += c[0] * PgMW + c[1]\n\n    # Verify feasibility\n    # Compute power mismatches\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * solution['Va'] * np.pi / 180)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator injection at buses\n    S_gen = np.zeros(len(buses), dtype=complex)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n    P_mismatch = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_mismatch = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n    max_P_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(P_mismatch))\n    max_Q_mismatch = np.max(np.abs(Q_mismatch))\n\n    # Voltage violations\n    v_violations = []\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] or Vm[i] > bus['vmax']:\n            v_violations.append(abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmin']) if Vm[i] < bus['vmin'] else abs(Vm[i] - bus['vmax']))\n    max_v_violation = max(v_violations) if v_violations else 0.0\n\n    # Branch overloads\n    overloads = []\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['flow_from_MVA'] > bf['limit_MVA']:\n            overloads.append(bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n    max_overload = max(overloads) if overloads else 0.0\n\n    # Build report\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n            \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n            \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n            \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n            \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_losses, 2),\n            \"solver_status\": solution['status']\n        },\n        \"generators\": [],\n        \"buses\": [],\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": [],\n        \"feasibility_check\": {\n            \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": round(max_P_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": round(max_Q_mismatch, 6),\n            \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": round(max_v_violation, 6),\n            \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": round(max_overload, 6)\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Add generators\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        report[\"generators\"].append({\n            \"id\": gen['id'],\n            \"bus\": gen['bus'],\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg[k], 4),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg[k], 4),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['Pmax'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 4),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 4)\n        })\n\n    # Add buses\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        report[\"buses\"].append({\n            \"id\": bus['id'],\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm[i], 6),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va[i], 4),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['vmax']\n        })\n\n    # Sort branches by loading and add top 10 most loaded\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)[:10]\n    for bf in sorted_branches:\n        report[\"most_loaded_branches\"].append({\n            \"from_bus\": int(bf['from_bus']),\n            \"to_bus\": int(bf['to_bus']),\n            \"loading_pct\": 71→    return branches\n    72→\n    73→def build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    74→    n_buses = len(buses)\n    75→    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    76→    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n    77→\n    78→    for branch in branches:\n    79→        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n    80→        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n    81→\n    82→        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n    83→        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n    84→        tap = branch['ratio']\n    85→\n    86→        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n    87→            angle = branch['angle']\n    88→            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n    89→            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_complex * np.conj(tap_complex)) + y_shunt\n    90→ 95→            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n    96→            ybus[f, t] -= y\n    97→            ybus[t, f] -= y\n    98→\n    99→    return ybus\n   100→\n   101→def newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=100, tol=1e-8):\n   102→    \"\"\"Full Newton-Raphson power flow with generator voltage/reactive support\"\"\"\n   103→    n_buses = len(buses)\n   104→    n_gens = len(generators)\n   105→\n   106→    # Bus identification\n   107→    ref_bus_idx = None\n   108→    PV_buses = []\n   109→    PQ_buses = []\n   110→\n   111→    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   112→        if bus['type'] == 3:\n   113→            ref_bus_idx = i\n   114→        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n   115→            PV_buses.append(i)\n   116→        else:\n   117→ = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n   124→    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n   125→        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n   126→        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n   127→\n   128→    # Initial voltages\n   129→    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n   130→    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n   131→    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n   132→\n   133→    # Initialize Pg, Qg\n   134→    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n   135→    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n   136→\n   137→    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n   148→\n   149→    # Iteration\n   150→    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n   151→        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n   152→        S_inj = np.conj(V) * gen['bus'])\n   161→            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n   162→\n   163→        # Mismatches\n   164→        P_mismatch = []\n   165→        Q_mismatch = []\n   166→        state_P = np.real(S_gen[i])\n   172→                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n   173→                state_P.append(i)\n   174→\n   175→        for i in PQ_buses:\n   176→            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n   177→            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n   178→            state_Q.append(i)\n   179→\n   180→        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n   181→\n   182→        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n   183→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n   184→\n   185→        # Build Jacobian\n   186→        nP = len(P_mismatch)\n   187→        nQ = len(Q_mismatch)\n   188→        n = nP + nQ\n   189→\n   190→        if n == 0:\n   191→            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n   192→\n   193→        J = np.zeros((n, n))\n   194→        eps = 1e-8\n   195→\n   196→        for col in range(n):\n   197→            Va_p = Va.copy()\n   198→            Vm_p = Vm.copy()\n   199→\n   200→            if col < nP:\n   201→                idx = state_P[col]\n   202→                Va_p[idx] += eps\n   203→            else:\n   204→                idx = state_Q[col - nP]\n   205→                Vm_p[idx] +=  211→            Q_p = np.imag(S_p) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n   212→\n   213→            for row, bus_idx in enumerate(state_P):\n   214→                J[row, col] = (P_p[bus_idx] - (np.real(S_inj) + np.imag(S_gen))[bus_idx]) / eps\n   217→\n   218→        try:\n   219→            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n   220→\n   221→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P):\n   222→                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n   223→            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q):\n   224→                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n   225→\n   226→            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n   227→\n   228→            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n   229→                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n   230→\n   231→            # Update Qg for PV buses to maintain voltage\n   232→            for i in PV_buses:\n   233→                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n   234→                    V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n   235→                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_complex * np.conj(tap_complex)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_complex)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_complex\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=100, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"Full Newton-Raphson power flow with generator voltage/reactive support\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Bus identification\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    PV_buses = []\n    PQ_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n PV_buses else 0\n\n    # Generator at bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initialize Pg, Qg\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # PV bus voltage setpoint\n    for i in PV_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Update generator injections\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Mismatches\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_P = []\n        state_Q = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i])\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                state_P.append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n Build Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mismatch)\n        nQ = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n = nP + nQ\n\n        if n == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\n        J = np.zeros((n, n))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for col in range(n):\n            Va_p = Va.copy()\n            Vm_p = Vm.copy()\n\n            if col < nP:\n                idx = state_P[col]\n                Va_p[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_Q[col - nP]\n                Vm_p[idx] += eps\n\n            V_p = Vm_p * np.exp(1j * Va_p)\n            S_p = np.conj(V_p) *
