    J[row, col] = (P_p[bus_idx] - (np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen))[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for row, bus_idx in enumerate(state_Q):\n                J[nP + row, col] = (Q_p[bus_idx] - (np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen))[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n            # Update Qg for PV buses to maintain voltage\n            for i in PV_buses:\n                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                    V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                    Qg[k] -= V_error * 50\n                    Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n 'Pmin': gen['Pmin'], 'Pmax': gen['Pmax']})\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        dispatch = min(item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin'], remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * branches):\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        r = branch['r']\n        x = branch['x']\n        z = r + 1j * x\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n        b = branch['b']\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio !=   ybus[t, f] -= y / tap\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Newton-Raphson Power Flow\n    Returns voltage magnitudes and angles\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses =        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            PV_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        # Find a PV bus to be reference\n        if PV_buses:\n            ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n        else:\n            ref_bus_idx = 0\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Total load\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Qd = np.sum([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Generator dispatch based on economic dispatch (proportional to Pmax)\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    total_Pmax = 0\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n        total_Pmax += gen['Pmax']\n\n    # Initialize generation\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens := len(generators))\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        # Distribute load proportionally to generator capacity\n        share = gen['Pmax'] / total_Pmax if total_Pmax > 0 else 0\n        Pg[k] = total_Pd * share\n        Qg[k] = total_Pd * 0.1 * share  # Assume 0.1 power factor\n\n    # PV bus voltage control - for generators at PV buses, set voltage\n    for i in PV_buses:\n        for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[k]['Vg']\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate mismatches\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Calculate generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k in range(n_gens):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Power balance\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector (exclude reference bus)\n        # For PV buses, Q is not a state variable\n        # For reference bus, P and Q are specified\n\n        # For this implementation, we'll use a simplified approach\n        # Just solve for all non-ref buses P and Q\n\n        # Mismatch for PQ buses\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_indices = {'P': [], 'Q': []}\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_indices['P'].append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_indices['Q'].append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n\n        # Partial derivatives (numerical)\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            # Perturb voltage angle or magnitude\n            if j < n_P:\n                # Perturb angle\n                idx = state_indices['P'][j]\n                Va_plus = Va.copy()\n                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            else:\n                # Perturb magnitude\n                idx = state_indices['Q'][j - n_P]\n                Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n                V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va)\n                S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n                P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n                Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                    J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n                for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                    J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve linear system\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['P']):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_indices['Q']):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n\n            # Update ref bus angle\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple economic dispatch based on generator marginal costs\n    \"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate marginal cost for each generator at Pmin\n    marginal_costs = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            # Quadratic: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0 -> marginal cost = 2*c2*Pg + c1\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1] if len(c) >= 2 else c[0]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = c[0]\n        else:\n            mc = 10.0  # Default\n\n        # Adjust for Pmax capacity\n        capacity = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']\n        if capacity <= 0:\n            capacity = 0.001\n\n        marginal_costs.append({'mc': mc, 'idx': k, 'capacity': capacity})\n\n    # Sort by marginal cost\n    marginal_costs.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    # Dispatch generators\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining_load = total_Pd / baseMVA\n\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        dispatch = min(generators[k]['Pmax'] - generators[k]['Pmin'], remaining_load)\n        Pg[k] = generators[k]['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    # If still have load, redistribute (some generators at Pmax)\n    for item in marginal_costs:\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = generators[k]['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            dispatch = min(available, remaining_load)\n            Pg[k] += dispatch\n            remaining_load -= dispatch\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve AC OPF using a practical approach:\n    1. Run power flow to get feasible voltages\n    2. Economic dispatch for generator setpoints\n    3. Run power flow with new setpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Step 1: Initial power flow\n    print(\"  Step 1: Running initial power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_init, Qg_init, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    if not pf_converged:\n        print(\"  Warning: Initial power flow did not converge, using flat start\")\n\n    # Step 2: Economic dispatch\n    print(\"  Step 2: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses]) * baseMVA\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    # Step 3: Update Qg based on reactive power needs\n    print(\"  Step 3: Computing reactive power dispatch...\")\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n\n        # Q required to meet reactive demand\n        Q_required = Qd[bus_idx] - np.imag(S_inj[bus_idx])\n\n        # Also maintain generator voltage if PV bus\n        if buses[bus_idx]['type'] == 2:\n            # Simple reactive power support\n            Q_required = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required + 20))\n\n        Qg[k] = max(gen['Qmin'], min(gen['Qmax'], Q_required))\n\n    # Step 4: Final power flow with new setpoints\n    print(\"  Step 4: Final power flow solution...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_final, Qg_final, pf_converged = newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg, Qg\n    )\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, 'optimal' if pf_converged else 'infeasible'\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf_with_pq_generators(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, Pg_setpoint, Qg_setpoint, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Power flow with specified generator setpoints\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_bus_idx = None\n    PV_buses = []\n    PQ_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_bus_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            PV_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None and PV_buses:\n        ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = 0\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Use the provided setpoints\n    Pg = Pg_setpoint.copy()\n    Qg = Qg_setpoint.copy()\n\n    # Create bus-generators mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k in range(n_gens):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == generators[k]['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate mismatches\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n        # For PV buses, adjust Qg to maintain voltage\n        for i in PV_buses:\n            V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg'] if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n            if abs(V_error) > 0.001 and gen_at_bus[i]:\n                k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                Qg[k] -= V_error * 10  # Simple voltage control\n                Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n                S_gen[i] = Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n                Q_calc[i] = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_P_idx = []\n        state_Q_idx = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_P_idx.append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_Q_idx.append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian numerically\n        n_P = len(P_mismatch)\n        n_Q = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n_state = n_P + n_Q\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_plus = Va.copy()\n            Vm_plus = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < n_P:\n                idx = state_P_idx[j]\n                Va_plus[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_Q_idx[j - n_P]\n                Vm_plus[idx] += eps\n\n            V_plus = Vm_plus * np.exp(1j * Va_plus)\n            S_inj_plus = np.conj(V_plus) * (ybus @ V_plus) * baseMVA\n\n            P_calc_plus = np.real(S_inj_plus) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_calc_plus = np.imag(S_inj_plus) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n                J[i, j] = (P_calc_plus[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n                J[n_P + i, j] = (Q_calc_plus[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.lstsq(J, -mismatch, rcond=None)[0]\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P_idx):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q_idx):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[n_P + i]\n\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n        except:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n\n        tap_ratio = branch['ratio']\n        angle = branch['angle']\n\n        if tap_ratio != 0 and tap_ratio != 1.0:\n            tap = tap_ratio * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    # Load network data\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse data\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = parse_gen_data(data['gen'], numpy as np\nimport cmath\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n    buses = []\n    for row in bus_list:\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(row[0]),\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmax': row[11],\n            'vmin': row[12]\n        })\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_list, baseMVA):\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_list):\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(row[0]),\n            'Pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'Qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Vg': row[5],\n            'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA\n        })\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_list):\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        n = int(row[3])\n        costs.append({'n': n, 'c': [float(row[4 + i]) for i in range(n)]})\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_list, baseMVA):\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branches.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': row[4],\n            'rateA': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180\n        })\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen):\n    \"\"\"Compute power injection at all buses\"\"\"\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V)\n    P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n    return P_calc, Q_calc, S_inj\n\ndef full_newton_raphson(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=100, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Full Newton-Raphson power flow\n    State variables: Va at all non-ref buses, Vm at PQ buses\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initial states\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg based on load proportion\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses)\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # Flatten PV bus Qg to zero initially (will be controlled during iteration)\n    for k in range(n_gens):\n        Qg[k] = 0.0\n\n    # Fixed loads\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n\n        # Current generator injection\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Compute mismatches\n        P_calc, Q_calc, _ = compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        # P mismatch for all non-ref buses\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va_idx = []  # indices for angle state variables\n        state_vm_idx = []  # indices for magnitude state variables\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va_idx.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm_idx.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        # Numerical Jacobian\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va_idx[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm_idx[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_gen_trial = S_gen.copy()  # Generator injection fixed for Jacobian\n\n            P_trial, Q_trial, _ = compute_power_injection(V_trial, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen_trial)\n\n            # Fill Jacobian columns\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n            # PV bus voltage control: adjust Qg to maintain Vm\n            for i in pv_buses:\n                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                    V_target = generators[k]['Vg']\n                    V_error = Vm[i] - V_target\n                    if abs(V_error) > 0.001:\n                        Qg[k] -= V_error * 10.0\n                        Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch using merit order\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Newton-Raphson power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = full_newton_raphson(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n    buses = []\n    for row in bus_list:\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(row[0]),\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'Pd': row[2] / parse_gen_data(gen_list, baseMVA):\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_list):\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(row[0]),\n            'Pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'Qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Vg': row[5],\n            'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA\n        })\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_list):\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        n = int(row[3])\n        costs.append({'n': n, 'c': [float(row[4 + i]) for i in range(n)]})\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_list, baseMVA):\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branches.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen):\n    \"\"\"Compute power injection at all buses\"\"\"\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V)\n    P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n    return P_calc, Q_calc, S_inj\n\ndef full_newton_raphson(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=100, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Full Newton-Raphson power flow\n    State variables: Va at all non-ref buses, Vm at PQ buses\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initial states\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg based on load proportion\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses)\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # Flatten PV bus Qg to zero initially (will be controlled during iteration)\n    for k in range(n_gens):\n        Qg[k] = 0.0\n\n    # Fixed loads\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n\n        # Current generator injection\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Compute mismatches\n        P_calc, Q_calc, _ = compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        # P mismatch for all non-ref buses\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va_idx = []  # indices for angle state variables\n        state_vm_idx = []  # indices for magnitude state variables\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va_idx.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm_idx.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        # Numerical Jacobian\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va_idx[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm_idx[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_gen_trial = S_gen.copy()  # Generator injection fixed for Jacobian\n\n            P_trial, Q_trial, _ = compute_power_injection(V_trial, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen_trial)\n\n            # Fill Jacobian columns\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n            # PV bus voltage control: adjust Qg to maintain Vm\n            for i in pv_buses:\n                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                    V_target = generators[k]['Vg']\n                    V_error = Vm[i] - V_target\n                    if abs(V_error) > 0.001:\n                        Qg[k] -= V_error * 10.0\n                        Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch using merit order\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Newton-Raphson power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = full_newton_raphson(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    branch_flows = []\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap_c)**2 -\n                      y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap_c)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap_c) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n    print(f\"Buses: {len(buses)}, Generators: {len(generators)}, Branches: {len(branches)}\")\n\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC       PQ_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_bus_idx is None:\n        ref_bus_idx = PV_buses.pop(0) if PV_buses else 0\n\n    # Generator at bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initial voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Initialize Pg, Qg\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # PV bus voltage setpoint\n    for i in PV_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n        Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n        # Update generator injections\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k] + 1j * Qg[k]\n\n        # Mismatches\n        P_mismatch = []\n        Q_mismatch = []\n        state_P = []\n        state_Q = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_bus_idx:\n                P_calc = np.real(S_inj[i]) + Pd[i] - np.real(S_gen[i])\n                P_mismatch.append(P_calc)\n                state_P.append(i)\n\n        for i in PQ_buses:\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj[i]) + Qd[i] - np.imag(S_gen[i])\n            Q_mismatch.append(Q_calc)\n            state_Q.append(i)\n\n        mismatch = np.array(P_mismatch + Q_mismatch)\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mismatch)\n        nQ = len(Q_mismatch)\n        n = nP + nQ\n\n        if n == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\n        J = np.zeros((n, n))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for col in range(n):\n            Va_p = Va.copy()\n            Vm_p = Vm.copy()\n\n            if col < nP:\n                idx = state_P[col]\n                Va_p[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_Q[col - nP]\n                Vm_p[idx] += eps\n\n            V_p = Vm_p * np.exp(1j * Va_p)\n            S_p = np.conj(V_p) * (ybus @ V_p) * baseMVA\n\n            P_p = np.real(S_p) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_p = np.imag(S_p) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n\n            for row, bus_idx in enumerate(state_P):\n                J[row, col] = (P_p[bus_idx] - (np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen))[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for row, bus_idx in enumerate(state_Q):\n                J[nP + row, col] = (Q_p[bus_idx] - (np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen))[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_P):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_Q):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_bus_idx] = 0.0\n\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n            # Update Qg for PV buses to maintain voltage\n            for i in PV_buses:\n                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                    V_error = Vm[i] - generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                    Qg[k] -= V_error * 50\n                    Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False, P_mismatch, Q_mismatch\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({'idx': k, 'mc': mc, 'Pmin': gen['Pmin'], 'Pmax': gen['Pmax']})\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        dispatch = min(item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin'], remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    branch_flows = []\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_complex = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap_complex)**2 -\n                      y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap_complex)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap_complex) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n    buses = []\n    for row in bus_list:\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(row[0]),\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'vm': row[7],\n            'va': row[8],\n            'vmax': row[11],\n            'vmin': row[12]\n        })\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_list, baseMVA):\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_list):\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(row[0]),\n            'Pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'Qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Vg': row[5],\n            'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA\n        })\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_list):\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        n = int(row[3])\n        costs.append({'n': n, 'c': [float(row[4 + i]) for i in      'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': row[4],\n            'rateA': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180\n        })\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen):\n    \"\"\"Compute power injection at all buses\"\"\"\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V)\n    P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n    Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - np.imag(S_gen)\n    return P_calc, Q_calc, S_inj\n\ndef full_newton_raphson(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=100, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"\n    Full Newton-Raphson power flow\n    State variables: Va at all non-ref buses, Vm at PQ buses\n    \"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initial states\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses])\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg based on load proportion\n    total_Pd = enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # Flatten PV bus Qg to zero initially (will be controlled during iteration)\n    for k in range(n_gens):\n        Qg[k] = 0.0\n\n    # Fixed loads\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n\n        # Current generator injection\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in P_calc, Q_calc, _ = compute_power_injection(V, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen)\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        # P mismatch for all non-ref buses\n        # Q mismatch for PQ buses only\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va_idx = []  # indices for angle state variables\n        state_vm_idx = []  # indices for magnitude state variables\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va_idx.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm_idx.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        # Build Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        # Numerical Jacobian\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va_idx[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm_idx[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_gen_trial = S_gen.copy()  # Generator injection fixed for Jacobian\n\n            P_trial, Q_trial, _ = compute_power_injection(V_trial, ybus, Pd, Qd, S_gen_trial)\n\n            # Fill Jacobian columns\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        # Solve\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            # Update states\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va_idx):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm_idx):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], bus['vmin'], bus['vmax'])\n\n            # PV bus voltage control: adjust Qg to maintain Vm\n            for i in pv_buses:\n                if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                    k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                    V_target = generators[k]['Vg']\n                    V_error = Vm[i] - V_target\n                    if abs(V_error) > 0.001:\n                        Qg[k] -= V_error * 10.0\n                        Qg[k] = max(generators[k]['Qmin'], min(generators[k]['Qmax'], Qg[k]))\n\n        except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch using merit order\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Newton-Raphson power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = full_newton_raphson(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    branch_flows = []\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        y = 1.0 / (branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x'])\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap_c)**2 -\n                      y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap_c)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap_c) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        loading = np.abs(S_from) / branch['rateA'] * 100 if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'flow_from_MVA': np.abs(S_from),\n            'flow_to_MVA': np.abs(S_to),\n            'loading_pct': loading,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n    baseMVA = {len(branches)}\")\n\n    ybus = build_ybus(buses, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC Optimal Power Flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, status = solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA)\n\n    Va_deg = Va * 180 / np.pi\n    Pg_MW = Pg * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = Qg * baseMVA\n\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA)\n\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_MVAr)\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    total_losses = total_Pg - ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators,      branches.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"Newton-Raphson power flow with proper handling\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n      Va[i] = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        V_conj = np.conj(V)\n\n        # Check for NaN\n        if np.any(np.isnan(V)):\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n        # Compute injection\n        S_inj = V_conj * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n\n        # Mismatches\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va = []\n        state_vm = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        # Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_trial = np.conj(V_trial) * (ybus @ V_trial) * baseMVA\n\n            P_trial = np.real(S_trial) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_trial = np.imag(S_trial) + Qd\n\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], max(bus['vmin'], 0.5), min(bus['vmax'], 1.5))\n                if np.isnan(Vm[i]) or np.isinf(Vm[i]):\n                    Vm[i] = 1.0\n\n        except:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Newton-Raphson power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = newton_raphson_pf(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    # Calculate Qg from power flow solution\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Qg for generators based on reactive balance\n    Qg = np.zeros(len(generators))\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        Q_gen_needed = Qd[bus_idx] - np.imag(S_inj[bus_idx])\n        Qg[k] = max(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, min(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, Q_gen_needed))\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg / baseMVA, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    branch_flows = []\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        if abs(z) < 1e-12:\n            z = python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow (AC OPF) Solver\nBased on the mathematical model in math-model.md\nImplements AC OPF with Newton-Raphson power flow and economic dispatch\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef load_network(filepath):\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        return json.load(f)\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_list, baseMVA):\n    buses = []\n    for row in bus_list:\n        buses.append({\n            'id': int(row[0]),\n            'type': int(row[1]),\n            'Pd': row[2] / parse_gen_data(gen_list, baseMVA):\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_list):\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': int(row[0]),\n            'Pg': row[1] / baseMVA,\n            'Qg': row[2] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n            'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n            'Vg': row[5],\n            'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n            'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA\n        })\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_list):\n    costs = []\n    for row in gencost_list:\n        n = int(row[3])\n        costs.append({'n': n, 'c': [float(row[4 + i]) for i in range(n)]})\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_list, baseMVA):\n    branches = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_list):\n        branches.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'from_bus': int(row[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(row[1]),\n            'r': row[2],\n            'x': row[3],\n            'b': n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        if abs(z) < 1e-12:\n            z = 1e-12 + 1j * 1e-12\n\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"Newton-Raphson power flow with proper handling\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initialize\n    Vm = np.ones(n_buses)\n    Va = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        Vm[i] = bus['vm']\n        Va[i] = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        V_conj = np.conj(V)\n\n        # Check for NaN\n        if np.any(np.isnan(V)):\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n        # Compute injection\n        S_inj = V_conj * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n\n        # Mismatches\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va = []\n        state_vm = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        # Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_trial = np.conj(V_trial) * (ybus @ V_trial) * baseMVA\n\n            P_trial = np.real(S_trial) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_trial = np.imag(S_trial) + Qd\n\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], max(bus['vmin'], 0.5), min(bus['vmax'], 1.5))\n                if np.isnan(Vm[i]) or np.isinf(Vm[i]):\n                    Vm[i] = 1.0\n\n        except:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Newton-Raphson power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = newton_raphson_pf(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    # Calculate Qg from power flow solution\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    # Qg for generators based on reactive balance\n    Qg = np.zeros(len(generators))\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        Q_gen_needed = Qd[bus_idx] - np.imag(S_inj[bus_idx])\n        Qg[k] = max(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, min(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, Q_gen_needed))\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg / baseMVA, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches, ybus, Vm, Va, baseMVA):\n    branch_flows = []\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        Vf = Vm[f] * np.exp(1j * Va[f])\n        Vt = Vm[t] * np.exp(1j * Va[t])\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        if abs(z) < 1e-12:\n            z = 1e-12 + 1j * 1e-12\n\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 / np.abs(tap_c)**2 -\n                      y * Vf * np.conj(Vt) / np.conj(tap_c)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf) / tap_c) * baseMVA\n        else:\n            S_from = (y * np.abs(Vf)**2 - y * Vf * np.conj(Vt)) * baseMVA\n            S_to = (y * np.abs(Vt)**2 - y * Vt * np.conj(Vf)) * baseMVA\n\n        limit =
