row[3],\n            'b': row[4],\n            'rateA': row[5] / baseMVA,\n            'ratio': row[8],\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180\n        })\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef build_ybus_decoupled(buses, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build B matrices for decoupled power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            x = 1e-10  # Avoid division by zero\n\n        b_line = -1.0 / x\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            B[f, f] += b_line / (tap**2)\n            B[t, t] += b_line\n            B[f, t] -= b_line / tap\n            B[t, f] -= b_line / tap\n        else:\n            B[f, f] += b_line\n            B[t, t] += b_line\n            B[f, t] -= b_line\n            B[t, f] -= b_line\n\n    return B\n\ndef gauss_seidel_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=200, tol=1e-6):\n    \"\"\"Gauss-Seidel power flow - more robust for difficult cases\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initialize voltages\n    Vm = np.array([bus['vm'] for bus in buses], dtype=complex)\n    Va = np.array([bus['va'] * np.pi / 180 for bus in buses])\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    V[ref_idx] = buses[ref_idx]['vm'] * np.exp(1j * 0.0)\n\n    # Initialize Pg, Qg\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    Qg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    # Gauss-Seidel iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V_old = V.copy()\n        max_change = 0.0\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i == ref_idx:\n                V[i] = buses[i]['vm'] * np.exp(1j * 0.0)\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate current injection\n            S_inj = V[i] * np.conj(ybus[i, :] @ V) * baseMVA\n\n            # Generator injection\n            P_gen = 0\n            Q_gen = 0\n            for k in gen_at_bus[i]:\n                P_gen += Pg[k]\n                Q_gen += Qg[k]\n\n            # Load\n            Pd = buses[i]['Pd']\n            Qd = buses[i]['Qd']\n\n            # Mismatch\n            P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - P_gen\n            Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd - Q_gen\n\n            # Update voltage\n            if buses[i]['type'] == 2:  # PV bus\n                # P mismatch determines angle change\n                if abs(ybus[i, i]) > 1e-10:\n                    V_new = (P_calc + 1j * np.imag(V[i]) * np.abs(ybus[i, i])) / ybus[i, i] + V[i]\n                    V_new = np.abs(V_new)\n                    V_new = np.clip(V_new, buses[i]['vmin'], buses[i]['vmax'])\n                    V[i] = V_new * np.exp(1j * np.angle(V[i]))\n            else:  # PQ bus\n                if abs(ybus[i, i]) > 1e-10:\n                    S_needed = P_gen + Q_gen - (Pd + 1j * Qd)\n                    V_new = np.conj(S_needed) / (np.conj(ybus[i, :]) @ np.conj(V)) * baseMVA\n                    V[i] = V_new\n\n            # Track max change\n            max_change = max(max_change, abs(V[i] - V_old[i]))\n\n        # PV bus voltage control\n        for i in pv_buses:\n            if gen_at_bus[i]:\n                k = gen_at_bus[i][0]\n                V[i] = generators[k]['Vg'] * np.exp(1j * np.angle(V[i]))\n\n        if max_change < tol:\n            Vm = np.abs(V)\n            Va = np.angle(V)\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, True\n\n    Vm = np.abs(V)\n    Va = np.angle(V)\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg, False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch using merit order based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': k,\n            'mc': mc,\n            'Pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'Pmax': gen['Pmax']\n        })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, remaining)\n        Pg[k] = item['Pmin'] + dispatch\n        remaining -= dispatch\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - Pg[k]\n        if available > 0:\n            Pg[k] += min(available, remaining)\n            remaining -= min(available, remaining)\n\n    return Pg\n\ndef solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses)\n    Pg = economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd, baseMVA)\n\n    print(\"  Step 2: Gauss-Seidel power flow...\")\n    Vm, Va, Pg_out, Qg, converged = gauss_seidel_pf(\n        buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    # np.array([bus['Qd'] * baseMVA for bus in buses])\n\n    Qg = np.zeros(len(generators))\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(i for i, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] ==    })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\ndef main():\n    data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    buses = parse_bus_data(data['bus'], baseMVA)\n    generators = 'ratio': row[8],\n            'angle': row[9] * np.pi / 180\n        })\n    return branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), })\n\n    mc_list.sort(key=lambda x: x['mc'])\n\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n    remaining = total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = print(\"  Step 2: DC power flow for voltage angles...\")\n    Va, pf_converged = dc_pf(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA)\n\n    # Default voltage magnitudes (1.0 pu for most, Vg for PV buses)\n branches\n\ndef build_ybus(buses, branches):\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    ybus = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n    bus_ids = {bus['id']: i for i, bus in enumerate(buses)}\n\n    for branch in branches:\n        f = bus_ids[branch['from_bus']]\n        t = bus_ids[branch['to_bus']]\n\n        z = branch['r'] + 1j * branch['x']\n        if abs(z) < 1e-12:\n            z = 1e-12 + 1j * 1e-12\n\n        y = 1.0 / z\n        y_shunt = 1j * branch['b'] / 2.0\n        tap = branch['ratio']\n\n        if tap != 0 and tap != 1.0:\n            angle = branch['angle']\n            tap_c = tap * np.exp(1j * angle)\n            ybus[f, f] += y / (tap_c * np.conj(tap_c)) + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y\n            ybus[f, t] -= y / np.conj(tap_c)\n            ybus[t, f] -= y / tap_c\n        else:\n            ybus[f, f] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[t, t] += y + y_shunt\n            ybus[f, t] -= y\n            ybus[t, f] -= y\n\n    return ybus\n\ndef newton_raphson_pf(buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA, max_iter=50, tol=1e-8):\n    \"\"\"Newton-Raphson power flow with proper handling\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Identify bus types\n    ref_idx = None\n    pv_buses = []\n    pq_buses = []\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        if bus['type'] == 3:\n            ref_idx = i\n        elif bus['type'] == 2:\n            pv_buses.append(i)\n        else:\n            pq_buses.append(i)\n\n    if ref_idx is None and pv_buses:\n        ref_idx = pv_buses.pop(0)\n    elif ref_idx is None:\n        ref_idx = 0\n\n    # Generator-bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = {i: [] for i in range(n_buses)}\n    for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n        gen_at_bus[bus_idx].append(k)\n\n    # Initialize\n    Vm = np.ones(n_buses)\n    Va = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n    for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n        Vm[i] = bus['vm']\n        Va[i] = bus['va'] * np.pi / 180\n\n    # Set PV bus voltages\n    for i in pv_buses:\n        if gen_at_bus[i]:\n            Vm[i] = generators[gen_at_bus[i][0]]['Vg']\n\n    # Initialize Pg\n    total_Pd = np.sum([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    total_Pmax = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators)\n    Pg = np.zeros(n_gens)\n\n    if total_Pmax > 0:\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            Pg[k] = gen['Pmin'] + (gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']) * (total_Pd / total_Pmax)\n\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] for bus in buses])\n    Qd = np.array([bus['Qd'] for bus in buses])\n\n    # Iteration\n    for iteration in range(max_iter):\n        V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n        V_conj = np.conj(V)\n\n        # Check for NaN\n        if np.any(np.isnan(V)):\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n        # Compute injection\n        S_inj = V_conj * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n\n        # Generator injection at buses\n        S_gen = np.zeros(n_buses, dtype=complex)\n        for k, gen in enumerate(generators):\n            bus_idx = next(j for j, b in enumerate(buses) if b['id'] == gen['bus'])\n            S_gen[bus_idx] += Pg[k]\n\n        # Mismatches\n        P_calc = np.real(S_inj) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n        Q_calc = np.imag(S_inj) + Qd\n\n        # Build mismatch vector\n        P_mis = []\n        Q_mis = []\n        state_va = []\n        state_vm = []\n\n        for i in range(n_buses):\n            if i != ref_idx:\n                P_mis.append(P_calc[i])\n                state_va.append(i)\n\n        for i in pq_buses:\n            Q_mis.append(Q_calc[i])\n            state_vm.append(i)\n\n        P_mis = np.array(P_mis)\n        Q_mis = np.array(Q_mis)\n        mismatch = np.concatenate([P_mis, Q_mis])\n\n        if np.max(np.abs(mismatch)) < tol:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        # Jacobian\n        nP = len(P_mis)\n        nQ = len(Q_mis)\n        n_state = nP + nQ\n\n        if n_state == 0:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), True\n\n        J = np.zeros((n_state, n_state))\n        eps = 1e-8\n\n        for j in range(n_state):\n            Va_trial = Va.copy()\n            Vm_trial = Vm.copy()\n\n            if j < nP:\n                idx = state_va[j]\n                Va_trial[idx] += eps\n            else:\n                idx = state_vm[j - nP]\n                Vm_trial[idx] += eps\n\n            V_trial = Vm_trial * np.exp(1j * Va_trial)\n            S_trial = np.conj(V_trial) * (ybus @ V_trial) * baseMVA\n\n            P_trial = np.real(S_trial) + Pd - np.real(S_gen)\n            Q_trial = np.imag(S_trial) + Qd\n\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                J[i, j] = (P_trial[bus_idx] - P_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n            for i, bus_idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                J[nP + i, j] = (Q_trial[bus_idx] - Q_calc[bus_idx]) / eps\n\n        try:\n            delta = np.linalg.solve(J, -mismatch)\n\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_va):\n                Va[idx] += delta[i]\n            for i, idx in enumerate(state_vm):\n                Vm[idx] += delta[nP + i]\n\n            Va[ref_idx] = 0.0\n\n            # Clip voltages\n            for i, bus in enumerate(buses):\n                Vm[i] = np.clip(Vm[i], max(bus['vmin'], 0.5), min(bus['vmax'], 1.5))\n                if np.isnan(Vm[i]) or np.isinf(Vm[i]):\n                    Vm[i] = 1.0\n\n        except:\n            return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, np.zeros(n_gens), False\n\ndef economic_dispatch(buses, generators, gencosts, total_Pd_MW, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Economic dispatch based on marginal costs\"\"\"\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n    total_Pd_pu = total_Pd_MW / baseMVA\n\n    mc_list = []\n    for k, (gen, cost) in enumerate(zip(generators, gencosts)):\n        if cost['n'] >= 3:\n            c = cost['c']\n            mc = 2 * c[0] * gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA + c[1]\n        elif cost['n'] == 2:\n            mc = cost['c'][0]\n        else:\n            mc = 20.0\n\n        mc_list.append({\n            'idx': total_Pd_pu\n\n    for item in mc_list:\n        if remaining <= 0:\n            break\n        k = item['idx']\n        available = item['Pmax'] - item['Pmin']\n        dispatch = min(available, solve_acopf(buses, generators, gencosts, branches, ybus, baseMVA):\n    \"\"\"Solve AC OPF\"\"\"\n    print(\"  Step 1: Economic dispatch...\")\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] * baseMVA for bus in     buses, generators, ybus, baseMVA\n    )\n\n    # Calculate Qg from power flow solution\n    V = Vm * np.exp(1j * Va)\n    S_inj = np.conj(V) * (ybus @ V) * baseMVA\n    Pd = np.array([bus['Pd'] * baseMVA, min(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, Q_gen_needed))\n\n    status = 'optimal' if converged else 'infeasible'\n\n    return Vm, Va, Pg, Qg / baseMVA, status\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(buses, branches,
